17e:T617,import { Graph } from '@antv/g6-lite';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    // #region graph-data
    data: {
      nodes: [
        { id: 'web', x: 100, y: 120, comboId: 'client' },
        { id: 'mobile', x: 220, y: 120, comboId: 'client' },
        { id: 'old-a', x: 420, y: 100, comboId: 'archive' },
        { id: 'old-b', x: 500, y: 140, comboId: 'archive' },
        { id: 'outside', x: 300, y: 260 },
      ],
      edges: [
        { id: 'live', source: 'mobile', target: 'outside' },
        { id: 'archived', source: 'old-a', target: 'outside' },
      ],
      combos: [
        { id: 'platform', data: { name: 'Platform' }, style: { padding: 24 } },
        { id: 'client', parentId: 'platform', data: { name: 'Client (selected)' }, states: ['selected'] },
        { id: 'archive', collapsed: true, data: { name: 'Archive (collapsed)' } },
      ],
    },
    // #endregion graph-data
    node: { style: { size: 22, label: (node) => node.id } },
    edge: { style: { lineWidth: 2, endArrow: true } },
    combo: {
      style: {
        padding: 30,
        label: (combo) => String(combo.data?.name ?? combo.id),
        labelPlacement: 'top',
        collapsedType: 'rect',
        collapsedSize: [150, 44],
        stateStyles: { selected: { stroke: '#873bf4', strokeWidth: 2 } },
      },
    },
    behaviors: [],
    autoFit: { padding: 56, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  try {
    await graph.render();
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}

19b:Te30,import { Graph } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: [
        { id: 'A', x: 80, y: 100, data: { value: 16, group: 'core' } },
        { id: 'B', x: 240, y: 100, data: { value: 28, group: 'other' } },
        { id: 'C', x: 400, y: 100, data: { value: 40, group: 'core' } },
      ],
      edges: [{ source: 'A', target: 'B' }, { source: 'B', target: 'C' }],
    },
    // #region node-options
    node: {
      type: 'circle',
      style: {
        size: (node) => Number(node.data?.value ?? 24),
        fill: (node) => node.data?.group === 'core' ? '#1783FF' : '#61DDAA',
        label: (node) => `${node.id}: ${node.data?.value}`,
        labelPlacement: 'bottom',
        labelOffsetY: 6,
      },
      stateStyles: {
        selected: { stroke: '#163B65', strokeWidth: 3, haloWidth: 12 },
      },
    },
    // #endregion node-options
    edge: { style: { stroke: '#94a3b8' } },
    autoFit: { padding: 40, maxZoom: 1 },
    behaviors: ['click-select'],
  });
  const ui = createDemoPanel(container);
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => {
    ui.destroy();
    destroy();
  };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    ui.addAction('B: 28 / 40', () => {
      const value = graph.getNodeData('B')?.data?.value === 28 ? 40 : 28;
      graph.updateNodeData('B', { value });
    });
    ui.addAction('Fill', () => {
      graph.updateOptions({ node: { style: { fill: '#7262FD' } } });
    });
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}

8b:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-edge","children":"使用示例"}]
1a2:T703,import { Graph } from '@antv/g6-lite';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const rows = [
    { id: 'Plain', y: 60, width: 1.5, color: '#64748b', dash: [], start: false, end: false, opacity: 1 },
    { id: 'Dash', y: 150, width: 3, color: '#f6903d', dash: [8, 8], start: false, end: true, opacity: 0.7 },
    { id: 'Both', y: 240, width: 5, color: '#1783FF', dash: [], start: true, end: true, opacity: 1 },
  ];
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: rows.flatMap((row) => [
        { id: `${row.id}-source`, x: 100, y: row.y, data: { label: row.id } },
        { id: `${row.id}-target`, x: 440, y: row.y },
      ]),
      edges: rows.map((row) => ({
        id: row.id, source: `${row.id}-source`, target: `${row.id}-target`, data: row,
      })),
    },
    node: { style: { size: 20, label: (node) => String(node.data?.label ?? '') } },
    // #region edge-options
    edge: {
      type: 'line',
      style: {
        stroke: (_source, _target, edge) => String(edge.data?.color ?? '#99ADD1'),
        lineWidth: (_source, _target, edge) => Number(edge.data?.width ?? 1),
        lineDash: (_source, _target, edge) => (edge.data?.dash as number[]) ?? [],
        opacity: (_source, _target, edge) => Number(edge.data?.opacity ?? 1),
        startArrow: (_source, _target, edge) => edge.data?.start === true,
        endArrow: (_source, _target, edge) => edge.data?.end === true,
        arrowSize: 16,
        hitAreaWidth: 14,
      },
      stateStyles: { selected: { stroke: '#873bf4', lineWidth: 6 } },
    },
    // #endregion edge-options
    behaviors: ['click-select'],
    autoFit: { padding: 48, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  try {
    await graph.render();
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const groups = [{ id: 'global', x: 100 }, { id: 'local', x: 320 }, { id: 'callback', x: 540 }];
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: groups.flatMap(({ id, x }) => [
        { id: id + '-a', x, y: 100, comboId: id },
        { id: id + '-b', x, y: 160, comboId: id },
      ]),
      edges: [],
      combos: [
        { id: 'global', data: { name: 'Global' } },
        { id: 'local', data: { name: 'Local' }, style: {
          fill: 'rgba(246,144,61,0.15)', stroke: '#f6903d', strokeWidth: 3,
          opacity: 0.75, borderRadius: 0, padding: 32,
          labelFontSize: 14, labelColor: '#7c2d12', labelPlacement: 'inside',
        } },
        { id: 'callback', style: {
          fill: (combo) => combo.childNodeIds.size >= 2 ? 'rgba(135,59,244,0.12)' : 'transparent',
          stroke: '#873bf4', borderRadius: 20, padding: [8, 40, 12, 24],
          label: (combo) => 'Members: ' + combo.childNodeIds.size, labelPlacement: 'bottom',
        } },
      ],
    },
    node: { style: { size: 22 } },
    // #region combo-options
    combo: { style: {
      fill: 'rgba(23,131,255,0.08)', stroke: '#1783FF', strokeWidth: 2,
      opacity: 1, borderRadius: 12, padding: 40,
      label: (combo) => String(combo.data?.name ?? combo.id),
      labelFontSize: 12, labelColor: '#1e3a8a', labelPlacement: 'top',
    } },
    // #endregion combo-options
    behaviors: [],
    autoFit: { padding: 56, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  try {
    await graph.render();
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}

c7:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-label","children":"使用示例"}]
1aa:Td6a,import { Graph, type LabelOptions } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  // #region label-options
  const policy: Required<LabelOptions> = {
    scaleWithZoom: false,
    hideOnLowZoom: true,
    hideOnCollision: true,
    nodeMinZoom: 0.3,
    edgeMinZoom: 0.5,
  };
  // #endregion label-options
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: [
        { id: 'Client alpha', x: 0, y: 100 },
        { id: 'Client beta', x: 100, y: 100 },
        { id: 'API gateway', x: 900, y: 100 },
      ],
      edges: [{ id: 'Request', source: 'Client beta', target: 'API gateway' }],
    },
    node: { style: { size: 48, label: (node) => node.id, labelFontSize: 14 } },
    edge: { style: { label: (_s, _t, edge) => edge.id ?? '', labelFontSize: 14 } },
    label: policy,
    behaviors: [],
    autoFit: { minZoom: 0.4, maxZoom: 0.4 },
  });
  const ui = createDemoPanel(container);
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => { ui.destroy(); destroy(); };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    const settings = { ...policy };
    for (const key of ['scaleWithZoom', 'hideOnLowZoom', 'hideOnCollision'] as const) {
      ui.gui.add(settings, key).onChange((checked: boolean) => {
        graph.updateOptions({ label: { [key]: checked } });
      });
    }
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}

ee:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-layout","children":"使用示例"}]
1bc:Ted7,import { Graph, type LayoutConfig } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  // #region layout-options
  const layouts: Record<string, LayoutConfig> = {
    grid: { type: 'grid', rows: 2, cols: 3, width: 360, height: 160 },
    circular: { type: 'circular', radius: 100 },
    dagre: { type: 'dagre', rankdir: 'LR', nodesep: 64, ranksep: 96, nodeSize: 28 },
  };
  // #endregion layout-options
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F'].map((id) => ({ id })),
      edges: [
        { source: 'A', target: 'B' }, { source: 'A', target: 'C' },
        { source: 'B', target: 'D' }, { source: 'C', target: 'E' },
        { source: 'D', target: 'F' }, { source: 'E', target: 'F' },
      ],
    },
    layout: layouts.grid,
    node: { style: { size: 28, label: (node) => node.id, labelOffsetY: 8 } },
    behaviors: [],
    autoFit: { padding: 56, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  const ui = createDemoPanel(container);
  let generation = 0;
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => { generation++; ui.destroy(); destroy(); };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    ui.setStatus('grid ready');
    ui.addChoice('Layout', Object.keys(layouts), 'grid', async (value) => {
      const current = ++generation;
      ui.setStatus('Running…');
      graph.setLayout(layouts[value]);
      try {
        await graph.layout();
        if (current !== generation || graph.destroyed) return;
        graph.fitView({ padding: 56, maxZoom: 1 });
        ui.setStatus(`${value} ready`);
      } catch {
        if (current === generation && !graph.destroyed) ui.setStatus('Layout failed');
      }
    });
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}

103:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-behaviors","children":"使用示例"}]
1ce:T711,import { Graph, type BehaviorName, type BuiltInBehaviorConfig } from '@antv/g6-lite';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  // #region behavior-options
  const behaviors = [
    { type: 'zoom-canvas', minZoom: 0.5, maxZoom: 2 },
    'drag-node',
    { type: 'click-select', multiple: true, trigger: 'shift' },
    { type: 'brush-select', key: null },
  ] satisfies Array<BehaviorName | BuiltInBehaviorConfig>;
  // #endregion behavior-options
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: {
      nodes: ['A', 'B', 'C', 'D', 'E', 'F', 'G', 'H'].map((id, i) => ({
        id, x: (i % 4) * 120, y: Math.floor(i / 4) * 100,
      })),
      edges: [{ id: 'A-B', source: 'A', target: 'B' }, { id: 'D-H', source: 'D', target: 'H' }],
    },
    node: {
      style: { size: 30, label: (node) => node.id, labelOffsetY: 8 },
      stateStyles: { selected: { fill: '#873BF4', stroke: '#5B21B6', strokeWidth: 2, haloWidth: 12 } },
    },
    behaviors,
    autoFit: { padding: 60, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  const status = document.createElement('div');
  status.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:20;background:white;color:#334155;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:6px;font:12px system-ui;';
  status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
  const updateStatus = () => {
    status.textContent = `selected nodes: ${graph.findNodesByState('selected').join(', ') || 'none'}`;
  };
  graph.on('node:selectchange', updateStatus);
  graph.on('selection:clear', updateStatus);
  graph.on('brush:end', updateStatus);
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => { status.remove(); destroy(); };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    updateStatus();
    container.append(status);
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}

136:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-theme","children":"使用示例"}]
1d9:Tecc,import { Graph, resolveTheme, type ThemeOption } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  // #region theme-options
  const themes: Record<string, ThemeOption> = {
    light: 'light',
    dark: 'dark',
    brand: {
      type: 'light', nodeFill: '#00A876', nodeSize: 36,
      edgeStroke: '#00A876', edgeLineWidth: 2, canvasBackground: '#F1F8F5',
      nodeStateStyles: {
        ...resolveTheme('light').nodeStateStyles,
        selected: { stroke: '#005C41', strokeWidth: 3, haloWidth: 12 },
      },
    },
  };
  // #endregion theme-options
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    theme: themes.light,
    data: {
      nodes: [
        { id: 'Default', x: 0, y: 0 },
        { id: 'Selected', x: 160, y: 0, states: ['selected'] },
        { id: 'Disabled', x: 320, y: 0, states: ['disabled'] },
      ],
      edges: [{ source: 'Default', target: 'Selected' }, { source: 'Selected', target: 'Disabled' }],
    },
    node: { style: { label: (node) => node.id, labelColor: '#334155', labelOffsetY: 8 } },
    behaviors: [],
    autoFit: { padding: 56, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  const ui = createDemoPanel(container);
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => { ui.destroy(); destroy(); };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    ui.addChoice('Theme', Object.keys(themes), 'light', (value) => {
      graph.updateOptions({
        theme: themes[value],
        node: { style: { labelColor: value === 'dark' ? '#E2E8F0' : '#334155' } },
      });
      // Keep non-white theme backgrounds accurate inside the dotted docs canvas.
      graph.getCanvas().style.mixBlendMode = value === 'light' ? 'multiply' : 'normal';
    });
    return graph;
  } catch (error) {
    graph.destroy();
    throw error;
  }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}

1e9:T1339,import { Graph, register, type GraphData, type TransformExtension } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

type Phase = 'init' | 'update' | 'run' | 'destroy';
interface NormalizeOptions { prefix?: string; onPhase?: (phase: Phase) => void }
// #region transform
export function createNormalize(): TransformExtension<NormalizeOptions> {
  let prefix = 'Svc';
  let report: NormalizeOptions['onPhase'];
  const configure = (options: Readonly<Partial<NormalizeOptions>>) => {
    prefix = options.prefix ?? 'Svc';
    report = options.onPhase;
  };
  return {
    init(_context, options) { configure(options); report?.('init'); },
    update(options) { configure(options); report?.('update'); },
    beforeRender(data) {
      report?.('run');
      return { ...data, nodes: data.nodes.map((node) => ({
        ...node, data: { ...node.data, label: prefix + ': ' + String(node.data?.name ?? node.id) },
      })) };
    },
    destroy() { report?.('destroy'); report = undefined; },
  };
}
register('transform', 'docs-normalize', () => createNormalize());
// #endregion transform

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const counters: Record<Phase, number> = { init: 0, update: 0, run: 0, destroy: 0 };
  const status = document.createElement('div');
  status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
  status.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;bottom:12px;left:12px;font:12px system-ui;color:#334155;background:white;padding:4px;';
  const updateStatus = () => {
    status.textContent = 'init=' + counters.init + ' update=' + counters.update + ' run=' + counters.run;
  };
  let revision = 0;
  const input = (): GraphData => ({
    nodes: [
      { id: 'A', x: 0, y: 0, data: { name: revision % 2 ? 'New API' : 'API' } },
      { id: 'B', x: 240, y: 0, data: { name: 'DB' } },
    ],
    edges: [{ id: 'A-B', source: 'A', target: 'B' }],
  });
  const graph = new Graph({
    container, data: input(),
    // #region config-transforms
    transforms: [{ type: 'docs-normalize', options: { prefix: 'Svc', onPhase: (phase: Phase) => { counters[phase]++; updateStatus(); } } }],
    // #endregion config-transforms
    node: { style: { size: 32, label: (node) => String(node.data?.label ?? node.id) } },
    behaviors: ['zoom-canvas', 'drag-canvas'],
    autoFit: { padding: 64, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  const ui = createDemoPanel(container);
  const destroy = graph.destroy.bind(graph);
  graph.destroy = () => { ui.destroy(); status.remove(); destroy(); };
  try {
    await graph.render();
    updateStatus();
    ui.addAction('Reload data', () => {
      revision++;
      void graph.setData(input(), { fitView: false }).catch((error) => { status.textContent = String(error); });
    });
    ui.addAction('Update name', () => {
      graph.updateNodeData('A', { name: 'Raw name' });
      updateStatus();
    });
    container.append(status);
    return graph;
  }
  catch (error) { graph.destroy(); throw error; }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}

161:["$","$L1e",null,{"as":"h3","id":"config-example-plugins","children":"使用示例"}]
1ee:Tfc7,import { Graph, register, type PluginExtension, type PluginOptions } from '@antv/g6-lite';
import GUI from 'lil-gui';

const NAME = 'docs-counter' as const;
interface CounterOptions extends PluginOptions<typeof NAME> {
  caption?: string;
  subscribe?: (listener: () => void) => () => void;
  onRenderAgain?: () => void;
}
// #region plugin
export function createCounter(): PluginExtension<CounterOptions> {
  let ui: ReturnType<typeof createDemoPanel> | undefined;
  let unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;
  let settings: CounterOptions | undefined;
  let count = 0;
  const report = () => ui?.setStatus(`${settings?.caption ?? 'Clicks'}: ${count}`);
  const increment = () => { count++; report(); };
  return {
    init(_context, options) { settings = options; },
    mount(container) {
      ui = createDemoPanel(container);
      ui.addAction('Plugin +1', increment);
      if (settings?.onRenderAgain) ui.addAction('Render again', settings.onRenderAgain);
      report();
      unsubscribe = settings?.subscribe?.(increment);
    },
    destroy() {
      unsubscribe?.(); unsubscribe = undefined;
      ui?.destroy();
      ui = undefined;
      settings = undefined;
    },
  };
}
register('plugin', NAME, () => createCounter());
// #endregion plugin

export async function run(container: HTMLElement) {
  const graph = new Graph({
    container,
    data: { nodes: [{ id: 'A', x: 0, y: 0 }, { id: 'B', x: 200, y: 0 }], edges: [] },
    node: { style: { size: 32, label: (node) => node.id } },
    // #region activate
    plugins: [{
      type: NAME, caption: 'Clicks',
      subscribe(listener: () => void) {
        graph.on('node:click', listener);
        return () => graph.off('node:click', listener);
      },
      onRenderAgain() { void graph.render().catch(console.error); },
    } satisfies CounterOptions],
    // #endregion activate
    behaviors: ['zoom-canvas', 'drag-canvas'],
    autoFit: { padding: 64, maxZoom: 1 },
  });
  try { await graph.render(); return graph; }
  catch (error) { graph.destroy(); throw error; }
}


export function createDemoPanel(container: string | HTMLElement, description = '') {
  const host = typeof container === 'string' ? document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(container)! : container;
  if (getComputedStyle(host).position === 'static') host.style.position = 'relative';

  let gui: GUI | undefined;
  const getGui = () => {
    if (!gui) {
      gui = new GUI({ container: host, width: 160 });
      gui.domElement.classList.add('g6lite-demo-controls');
      gui.domElement.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      Object.assign(gui.domElement.style, {
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '10px',
        right: '10px',
        zIndex: '30',
        maxWidth: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
        maxHeight: 'calc(100% - 20px)',
      });
    }
    return gui;
  };

  let status: HTMLDivElement | undefined;
  const setStatus = (message: string) => {
    if (!status) {
      status = document.createElement('div');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      status.dataset.role = 'status';
      status.dataset.captureIgnore = '';
      status.style.cssText =
        'position:absolute;left:12px;bottom:10px;z-index:20;max-width:min(28rem,calc(100% - 24px));color:#64748b;font:12px/1.45 system-ui,sans-serif;pointer-events:none;white-space:pre-wrap;';
      host.append(status);
    }
    status.textContent = message;
  };
  if (description) setStatus(description);

  return {
    get gui() {
      return getGui();
    },
    addAction(label: string, action: () => void, folder = getGui()) {
      return folder.add({ [label]: action }, label);
    },
    addChoice<T extends string>(
      label: string,
      choices: readonly T[] | Record<string, T>,
      value: T,
      onChange: (value: T) => void,
    ) {
      const options = Array.isArray(choices) ? [...choices] : choices;
      return getGui().add({ value }, 'value', options).name(label).onChange(onChange);
    },
    setStatus,
    destroy() {
      gui?.destroy();
      status?.remove();
    },
  };
}
